First row is delay time, second row is duration of delay period\n   126→        sample_time = sess_file['task_sample_time'] # ndarray, (2,n_trials). First row is sample time, second row is duration of sample period\n   127→        delay_period = delay_time[1,:] # only need duration of delay period\n   128→        sample_period = sample_time[1,:] # only need duration of sample period\n   129→        stimulation = sess_file['task_stimulation'] # ndarray, (n_trials,4). The 4 columns are [laser_power, stim_type('1', '2', or '6', with 1,2,6 being left/right/both ALM perturbation), laser_on_time, laser_off_time] (with reference to trial start time - need to subtract go cue time from it).\n   130→        trial_type = sess_file['task_trial_type'] # ndarray, (1,n_trials), 'l' or 'r'\n   131→\n   132→        # align stimulation time and lick times to gocue_time=0 #\n   133→        lick_time_cal = []\n   134→        for i_trial, lick_times_trial in enumerate(lick_times):\n   135→            gocue_time_trial = gocue_time[0, i_trial]\n   136→            lick_time_cal.append(lick_times_trial - gocue_time_trial)\n   137→            stimulation[i_trial, 2:] -= gocue_time_trial\n   138→        lick_times = lick_time_cal\n   139→\n   140→        if i_file == 0:\n   141→            ### only save behavior and task info for the first probe file ###\n   142→            sess_dict = {}\n   143→            sess_dict['sess_name'] = sess_name\n   144→            sess_dict['auto_learn_trials'] = auto_learn_trials\n   145→            sess_dict['early_lick_trials'] = early_lick_trials # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n   146→            sess_dict['auto_water_trials'] = auto_water_trials # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n   147→            sess_dict['free_water_trials'] = free_water_trials # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n   148→            sess_dict['lick_directions'] = lick_directions # ndarray, (n_trials). Each element is a list of 0 (lick right) and 1 (lick left)\n   149→            sess_dict['lick_times'] = lick_time_cal # list with length n_trials, each element is 1D ndarray with all lick times in that trial\n   150→            sess_dict['gocue_time'] = gocue_time[0,:]\n   151→            sess_dict['correctness'] = correctness # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64. 1: correct or free water, 0: error, -1: no response\n   152→            sess_dict['delay_period'] = delay_period # (n_trials,) duration of delay period\n   153→            sess_dict['sample_period'] = sample_period # (n_trials,) duration of sample period\n   154→            sess_dict['stimulation'] = stimulation # ndarray, (n_trials,4). The 4 columns are [laser_power, stim_type('1', '2', or '6', with 1,2,6 being left/right/both ALM perturbation), laser_on_time, laser_off_time]. For no stim trials, we have [0, NaN, NaN, NaN]\n   155→            sess_dict['trial_type'] = 1*(trial_type=='l') # ndarray, (n_trials,). 1 is left, 0 is right 'r'\n   156→            # with open(os.path.join(save_folder_sess, 'behavior_task.pickle'), 'wb') as f:\n   157→            #     pickle.dump(sess_dict, f)\n   158→\n   159→            ### Read the neuron IDs after QC for this session ###\n   160→            '''\n   161→            The index refers to within individual SESSION, resulting in summing all units from all penetrations in a given Session. For instance, if in Session 08/17/22, we have 3 penetrations: p1 has 10 units, p2 has 30 units, p3 has 5 units (before filtering) - overall there are 45 units. An index of '42' from this session means the 2nd (42-10-30) unit from p3.\n   162→            '''\n   163→            temp_list = list(glob.glob(os.path.join(qc_folder, '{}*.mat'.format(sess_name_qc))))\n   164→            assert len(temp_list) == 1\n   165→            filepath_qc = temp_list[0]\n   166→\n   167→            qc_file = preprocessing_utils.loadmat(filepath_qc)\n   168→            # idx_qc_dict = qc_file['Idx'] # dict. Keys are 'region_qc' (e.g. 'ALM_qc'). Values are list of good neuron IDs (in MATLAB it starts from 1 but here we change it to start from 0).\n   169→            idx_qc_dict = {}# dict. Keys are 'region_qc' (e.g. 'ALM_qc'). Values are list of good neuron IDs (in MATLAB it starts from 1 but here we change it to start from 0).\n   170→            for area, id_list in qc_file['Idx'].items():\n   171→                if id_list == []:\n   172→                    idx_qc_dict[area] = []\n   173→                elif isinstance(id_list, int) or isinstance(id_list, float): # only one neuron\n   174→                    idx_qc_dict[area] = [id_list-1]\n   175→                else:\n   176→                    idx_qc_dict[area] = list(np.array(id_list) - 1)\n   177→\n   178→            anno_qc_dict = {}\n   179→            for name, value in qc_file['AnnoName'].items():\n   180→                if isinstance(value, str):\n   181→                    anno_qc_dict[name] = [value]\n   182→                else:\n   183→                    anno_qc_dict[name] = value\n   184→\n   185→        # --- 1.2 neural data ---\n   186→        # -- 1.2.1 spike times\n   187→        spike_times = sess_file['neuron_single_units'] # ndarray. spike_times[neuron_id][trial_id] is ndarray\n   188→\n   189→        # -- 1.2.2 unit_info and QC stats\n   190→        unit_info = sess_file['neuron_unit_info'] # ndarray (n_units, 8)\n   191→        # neuron_unit_info: columns: unit_id, unit_quality, unit_x_in_um, depth_in_um, associated_electrode, shank, cell_type, recording_location. For putative type, we have: 'Pyr' - pyramidal neurons, 'FS' - fast spiking, 'not classified', 'all' - all types\n   192→        probe_no_col = probe * np.ones((unit_info.shape[0], 1)) # (n_units,1) original probe #\n   193→        unit_info = np.concatenate([unit_info, probe_no_col], axis=1) # (n_units, 9)\n   194→        unit_qc = sess_file['neuron_unit_quality_control']\n   195→        # dictionary. Needed keys: 'unit_amp', 'presence_ratio', 'amplitude_cutoff', 'isi_violation', 'unit_snr', 'avg_firing_rate', 'drift_metric'. Values are all list with length n_neurons.\n   196→\n   197→        # -- 1.2.3 histology\n   198→        histology = sess_file['histology'] # dictionary, keys: 'unit', 'ccf_x', 'ccf_y', 'ccf_z', 'annotation'\n   199→        ccf_unit_id = np.array(histology['unit']) # ndarray, (n_neurons,)\n   200→        ccf_coordinate = np.stack([histology['ccf_x'], histology['ccf_y'], histology['ccf_z']]).swapaxes(0,1) # ndarray (n_neurons,3), xyz coordinates\n   201→        ccf_label = histology['annotation'] # list of np.str_\n   202→        ccf_label = [str(x).strip() for x in ccf_label] # list of string, with both whitespace at the beginning and end of each string removed\n   203→\n   204→        # -- 1.2.4 get units that have both ephys and histology\n   205→        unit_comb = set(unit_info[:,0]) & set(ccf_unit_id) # get the id of units that have both ephys and histology\n   206→        mask_ephys = [True if x in unit_comb else False for x in unit_info[:,0]] # mask for all other data (ephys) except histology\n   207→        mask_histology =[True if x in unit_comb else False for x in ccf_unit_id] # mask for histology only\n   208→\n   209→        # mask CCF info\n   210→        ccf_coordinate = ccf_coordinate[mask_histology,:] # ndarray (n_neurons,3), xyz coordinates\n   211→        ccf_label = list(compress(ccf_label, mask_histology)) # list of string, with both whitespace at the beginning and end of each string removed\n   212→        ccf_unit_id = ccf_unit_id[mask_histology]\n   213→\n   214→        # mask ephys info\n   215→        unit_info = unit_info[mask_ephys,:] # (n_neurons, 9)\n   216→        spike_times = spike_times[mask_ephys]\n   217→        for name in unit_qc.keys():\n   218→            unit_qc[name] = list(np.array(unit_qc[name])[mask_ephys])\n   219→\n   220→        # --- 1.3 add probe data to sess data ---\n   221→        spike_times_sess.append(spike_times) # ndarray. spike_times[neuron_id][trial_id] is ndarray\n   222→        unit_info_sess.append(unit_info) # (n_units, 9)\n   223→        unit_qc_sess_list.append(unit_qc) # dictionary. Needed keys: 'unit_amp', 'presence_ratio', 'amplitude_cutoff', 'isi_violation', 'unit_snr', 'avg_firing_rate', 'drift_metric'. Values are all list with length n_neurons.\n   224→        ccf_coordinate_sess.append(ccf_coordinate) # ndarray (n_neurons,3), xyz coordinates\n   225→        ccf_label_sess = ccf_label_sess + ccf_label # list\n   226→        ccf_unit_id_sess.append(ccf_unit_id) # ndarray, (n_neurons,)\n   227→\n   228→    # ----- 2. combine data from all probes ---\n   229→    spike_times_sess = np.concatenate(spike_times_sess) # ndarray. spike_times[neuron_id][trial_id] is ndarray\n   230→    unit_info_sess = np.concatenate(unit_info_sess, axis=0) # (n_units, 9)\n   231→\n   232→    # uncommented\n   233→    unit_qc_sess = {'unit_amp': [], 'presence_ratio': [], 'amplitude_cutoff': [], 'isi_violation': [], 'avg_firing_rate':[], 'drift_metric': []}\n   234→    for probe_qc_dict in unit_qc_sess_list:\n   235→        for qc_name in unit_qc_sess.keys():\n   236→            unit_qc_sess[qc_name] = unit_qc_sess[qc_name] + probe_qc_dict[qc_name]\n   237→\n   238→    ccf_coordinate_sess = np.concatenate(ccf_coordinate_sess, axis=0) # ndarray (n_neurons,3), xyz coordinates\n   239→    ccf_unit_id_sess = np.concatenate(ccf_unit_id_sess) # ndarray, (n_neurons,)\n   240→\n   241→    n_neurons = len(spike_times_sess)\n   242→    assert unit_info_sess.shape[0] == n_neurons\n   243→    assert len(unit_qc_sess['drift_metric']) == n_neurons\n   244→    assert ccf_coordinate_sess.shape[0] == n_neurons\n   245→    assert len(ccf_label_sess) == n_neurons\n   246→    assert len(ccf_unit_id_sess) == n_neurons\n   247→    # print('total neurons:', n_neurons)\n   248→\n   249→    # ----- 3. Process neurons from areas of interest and perform QC filtering -----\n   250→    i_file = 1\n   251→    neuron_id_used = []\n   252→    neuron_id_all = np.arange(n_neurons) # neuron_id is continuous from 0. unit_id is the original id from DJ.\n   253→\n   254→    for region in ['ALM', 'Medulla', 'Midbrain', 'Striatum', 'Thalamus', 'Pons', 'Cerebellum', 'Hypothalamus', 'Hippocampus', 'Orbital', 'OtherCortex', 'Olfactory', 'CorticalSubplate', 'Pallidum']:\n   255→        for side in ['left', 'right']:\n   256→            area = '_'.join([side, region])\n   257→            area_key = '{}_qc'.format(region)\n   258→            neuron_id_area = helper_get_neuron_id_area(idx_qc_dict[area_key], anno_qc_dict[area_key], ccf_coordinate_sess, ccf_label_sess, side, region)\n   259→\n   260→            if len(neuron_id_area) == 0:\n   261→                continue\n   262→\n   263→            print('Filtered neurons:', area, len(neuron_id_area))\n   264→\n   265→            # record used neurons\n   266→            neuron_id_used = neuron_id_used + neuron_id_area\n   267→\n   268→            save_flag = process_one_area(sess_name, area, neuron_id_area, i_file, bw, stride, begin_time, end_time, save_folder_sess, spike_times_sess, unit_info_sess, unit_qc_sess, ccf_coordinate_sess, ccf_label_sess, ccf_unit_id_sess, sess_dict)\n   269→\n   270→            if save_flag:\n   271→                i_file += 1\n   272→\n   273→    # ----- 4. Don't process the rest of the neurons -----\n   274→    with open(os.path.join(save_folder_sess, 'others_finished.txt'), 'w') as f:\n   275→        f.write('{}: finished.'.format(sess_name))\n   276→\n   277→    '''\n   278→    neuron_id_others = set(neuron_id_all) - set(neuron_id_used)\n   279→    neuron_id_others = sorted(list(neuron_id_others))\n   280→\n   281→    if len(neuron_id_others)==0:\n   282→        return\n   283→\n   284→    print('Filtered neurons:', 'others', len(neuron_id_others))\n   285→    process_one_area(sess_name, 'others', neuron_id_others, i_file, bw, stride, begin_time, end_time, save_folder_sess, spike_times_sess, unit_info_sess, ccf_coordinate_sess, ccf_label_sess, ccf_unit_id_sess, sess_dict)\n   286→    '''\n   287→\n   288→\n   289→def process_one_area(sess_name, area, neuron_id_area, i_file, bw, stride, begin_time, end_time, save_folder_sess, spike_times_sess, unit_info_sess, unit_qc_sess, ccf_coordinate_sess, ccf_label_sess, ccf_unit_id_sess, sess_dict):\n   290→    \"\"\"\n   291→    Get all neurons in an area, extract firing rates, and then save data.\n   292→    \"\"\"\n   293→    if os.path.exists(os.path.join(save_folder_sess, '%d_%s.pickle' % (i_file, area))):\n   294→        return True\n   295→\n   296→    # filter using the neuron mask\n   297→    n_units = unit_info_sess.shape[0]\n   298→    spike_times_area = spike_times_sess[neuron_id_area]\n   299→    unit_info_area = unit_info_sess[neuron_id_area,:] # (n_units, 9)\n   300→\n   301→    # uncommented\n   302→    unit_qc_area = {}\n   303→    for qc_name in unit_qc_sess.keys():\n   304→        unit_qc_area[qc_name] = np.array(unit_qc_sess[qc_name])[neuron_id_area]\n   305→        assert len(unit_qc_sess[qc_name]) == n_units\n   306→\n   307→    ccf_coordinate_area = ccf_coordinate_sess[neuron_id_area,:] # ndarray (n_neurons,3)\n   308→    ccf_label_area = list(np.array(ccf_label_sess)[neuron_id_area])\n   309→    ccf_unit_id_area = list(ccf_unit_id_sess[neuron_id_area])\n   310→\n   311→    # truncate spike times into desired time window (already aligned to go cue in raw data)\n   312→    for i_cluster, st_cluster in enumerate(spike_times_area):\n   313→        for i_trial, st_trial in enumerate(st_cluster):\n   314→            # if spike_times[i_cluster][i_trial] != []:\n   315→            #     amin_all.append(np.amin(spike_times[i_cluster][i_trial]))\n   316→            #     amax_all.append(np.amax(spike_times[i_cluster][i_trial]))\n   317→            if isinstance(st_trial, float) or isinstance(st_trial, int):\n   318→                spike_times_area[i_cluster][i_trial] = np.array([st_trial])\n   319→            # temp = spike_times[i_cluster][i_trial].copy()\n   320→            temp = spike_times_area[i_cluster][i_trial] # in Susu's data, spike_times are relative to go cue time\n   321→            spike_times_area[i_cluster][i_trial] = spike_times_area[i_cluster][i_trial][(temp>=begin_time)*(temp<=end_time)]\n   322→\n   323→    # bin spike times into firing rates\n   324→    bin_centers, fr = sliding_histogram(spike_times_area, begin_time, end_time, bin_width=bw, stride=stride, rate=True) # fr: (n_bins, n_trials, n_neurons)\n   325→\n   326→    if os.path.exists(os.path.join(save_folder_sess, '%d_%s.pickle' % (i_file, area))):\n   327→        with open(os.path.join(save_folder_sess, '%d_%s.pickle' % (i_file, area)), 'rb') as f: # HERE\n   328→            area_dict_exist = pickle.load(f)\n   329→            fr = area_dict_exist['fr']\n   330→            spike_times_area = area_dict_exist['spike_times']\n   331→            bin_centers = area_dict_exist['bin_centers']\n   332→\n   333→    # organize data into dict\n   334→    area_dict = {}\n   335→    area_dict['sess_name'] = sess_name # str\n   336→    area_dict['area'] = area # str\n   337→\n   338→    area_dict['bin_centers'] = bin_centers\n   339→    area_dict['fr'] = fr # (n_bins, n_trials, n_neurons)\n   340→    area_dict['spike_times'] = spike_times_area # ndarray. spike_times[neuron_id][trial_id] is ndarray with all spike times of this neuron in this trial. (go cue time is 0.)\n   341→    area_dict['neuron_info'] = unit_info_area # ndarray (n_units, 9)\n   342→    #  columns: unit_id, unit_quality, unit_x_in_um, depth_in_um, associated_electrode, shank, cell_type, recording_location, probe #. For putative type, we have: 'Pyr' - pyramidal neurons, 'FS' - fast spiking, 'not classified', 'all' - all types\n   343→    area_dict['unit_qc'] = unit_qc_area # dict. Keys are 'unit_amp', 'presence_ratio', 'amplitude_cutoff', 'isi_violation',, 'avg_firing_rate', 'drift_metric'. Values are all list with length n_neurons.\n   344→    area_dict['ccf_coordinate'] = ccf_coordinate_area # ndarray (n_neurons,3), xyz coordinates\n   345→    area_dict['ccf_label'] = ccf_label_area # list of string, with whitespace at the beginning and end of each string removed\n   346→    area_dict['ccf_unit_id'] = ccf_unit_id_area # ndarray (n_neurons,), id of units with histology\n   347→\n   348→    for name, info in sess_dict.items():\n   349→        area_dict[name] = info\n   350→\n   351→    # save data\n   352→    with open(os.path.join(save_folder_sess, '%d_%s.pickle' % (i_file, area)), 'wb') as f:\n   353→        pickle.dump(area_dict, f)\n   354→\n   355→    return True\n   356→\n   357→\n   358→\n   359→def helper_get_neuron_id_area(neuron_idx_list, anno_qc_list, ccf_coor, ccf_label, side, region):\n   360→    \"\"\"\n   361→    Filter the neurons in data_sess so that only neurons in side_region is left.\n   362→\n   363→    Args:\n   364→        neuron_idx_list (list): list of good neuron IDs.\n   365→        anno_qc_list (list): list of CCF annotations of good neurons.\n   366→        ccf_coor (ndarray): ndarray (n_neurons,3), xyz coordinates.\n   367→        ccf_label (list): list of string (CCF annotations), with length n_neurons.\n   368→        side (str): 'left' or 'right'.\n   369→        region (str): 'ALM', 'Medulla', 'Midbrain', 'Striatum', 'Thalamus', 'BLA', or 'ECT'.\n   370→\n   371→    Returns:\n   372→        neuron_id_area (list): id of neurons within side_region.\n   373→    \"\"\"\n   374→    if len(neuron_idx_list) == 0:\n   375→        return []\n   376→\n   377→    n_neurons = ccf_coor.shape[0]\n   378→    assert len(ccf_label) == n_neurons\n   379→    neurons_all = np.arange(n_neurons)\n   380→\n   381→    # filter neurons using side\n   382→    ML_mid_coor = 5700\n   383→    if side == 'left':\n   384→        mask_side = ccf_coor[:,0] >= ML_mid_coor\n   385→    elif side == 'right':\n   386→        mask_side = ccf_coor[:,0] < ML_mid_coor\n   387→    neuron_id_side = neurons_all[mask_side]\n   388→\n   389→    if len(neuron_id_side) == 0:\n   390→        return []\n   391→\n   392→    # combine side filter with qc filter\n   393→    neuron_id_area = sorted(list(set(neuron_id_side) & set(neuron_idx_list)))\n   394→    neuron_id_area = [idx for idx in neuron_id_area if ccf_label[idx] != []]\n   395→    if len(neuron_id_area) == 0:\n   396→        return []\n   397→    ccf_label_list = list(np.array(ccf_label)[neuron_id_area])\n   398→    ccf_label_joined = ''.join(ccf_label_list)\n   399→\n   400→    idx_side = [i for i, idx in enumerate(neuron_idx_list) if idx in neuron_id_area]\n   401→    anno_qc_list = list(np.array(anno_qc_list)[idx_side])\n   402→    anno_qc_joined = ''.join(anno_qc_list)\n   403→\n   404→    # temp = list(np.array(ccf_label)[neuron_id_area])\n   405→    # print(len(temp), len(anno_qc_list))\n   406→    # num = np.amin([len(temp), len(anno_qc_list)])\n   407→    # for ii in range(num):\n   408→        # if temp[ii] != anno_qc_list[ii]:\n   409→        # print(ii, temp[ii], anno_qc_list[ii])\n   410→    assert ccf_label_joined == anno_qc_joined\n   411→\n   412→    '''\n   413→    # filter neurons using region\n   414→    if region == 'ALM':\n   415→        res = 100 # resolution: 100 um\n   416→        # ALM_voxels = np.load(open('ALM_voxels_raw.npy', 'rb')) # (n_voxels,3), xyz of ALM\n   417→        ALM_voxels = np.load(open('ALM_voxels_symmetric.npy', 'rb')) # (n_voxels,3), xyz of ALM regions\n   418→\n   419→        diff = ccf_coor[:,None,:] - ALM_voxels[None,:,:] # (n_neurons, n_voxels, 3)\n   420→        mask_region = (np.abs(diff[:,:,0]) <= res/2) * (diff[:,:,1] <= res/2) * (np.abs(diff[:,:,2]) <= res/2) # (n_neurons, n_voxels)\n   421→        mask_region = np.any(mask_region, axis=1) # (n_neurons,)\n   422→\n   423→        neuron_id_region = neurons_all[mask_region]\n   424→    else:\n   425→        mask_region, neuron_id_region = get_id_subarea(region, ccf_label)\n   426→    '''\n   427→\n   428→    # neuron_id_area = sorted(list( set(neuron_id_side) & set(neuron_id_region)))\n   429→    return neuron_id_area\n   430→\n   431→\n   432→def helper_filter_by_neuron_id(data_sess, neuron_id_area, probe_no, area_name):\n   433→    \"\"\"\n   434→    Using the given neuron_id_area, filter the neuron sin data_sess.\n   435→\n   436→    Arg:\n   437→        data_sess (dict): same format as sess_dict.\n   438→        neuron_id_area (list): list of neuron if to use.\n   439→        probe_no (int): probe number.\n   440→        area_name (str):  name of the area. e.g. 'left_ALM'.\n   441→\n   442→    Returns:\n   443→        data_area (dict): same format as sess_dict.\n   444→    \"\"\"\n   445→    data_area = {}\n   446→\n   447→    data_area['mouse'] = data_sess['mouse']\n   448→    data_area['date'] = data_sess['date']\n   449→    data_area['probe'] = probe_no\n   450→    data_area['area'] = area_name\n   451→\n   452→    data_area['bin_centers'] = data_sess['bin_centers']\n   453→    data_area['fr'] = data_sess['fr'][:,:,neuron_id_area] # (n_bins, n_trials, n_neurons)\n   454→    data_area['spike_times'] = data_sess['spike_times'][neuron_id_area] # ndarray. spike_times[neuron_id][trial_id] is ndarray with all spike times of this neuron in this trial. (go cue time is 0.)\n   455→    data_area['auto_learn_trials'] = data_sess['auto_learn_trials'] # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n   456→    data_area['early_lick_trials'] = data_sess['early_lick_trials'] # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n   457→    data_area['auto_water_trials'] = data_sess['auto_water_trials'] # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n   458→    data_area['free_water_trials'] = data_sess['free_water_trials'] # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n   459→    data_area['lick_directions'] = data_sess['lick_directions'] # ndarray, (n_trials). 247→\n   248→def temporal_alignment_embed_and_ephys(ephys_tt,embed_tt, embed_vecs, fr, dt = 0.0034):\n   249→    '''Align the embeding vectors and the firing rates in time.\n   250→    \n   251→    This assumes that the dt is the same for both datasets.\n   252→\n   253→    Parameters\n   254→    ----------\n   255→    ephys_tt : np.ndarray\n   256→        The time stamps for the ephys data.\n   257→    embed_tt : np.ndarray\n   258→        The time stamps for the embedding vectors.\n   259→    embed_vecs : np.ndarray [time, trial, embedding_dim]\n   260→        The embedding vectors.\n   261→    fr : np.ndarray [time, trial, neuron]\n   262→        The firing rates.\n   263→    dt : float, optional\n   264→        The time step. 177→\n   178→\n   179→def random_split_keep_ratio(x, y, test_ratio=0.2):\n   180→    \"\"\"\n   181→    Randomly splitting input x according to the first dimension, keeping the ratio of different  221→\n   222→def my_r2_score(y_true, y_pred):\n   223→    numerator = np.sum((y_pred - y_true)**2, axis=0)\n   224→    denominator = np.sum((y_true - np.average(y_true, axis=0))**2, axis=0)\n   225→ Both have the same t-score, but p_value(2-tailed) = 2*p_value(1-tailed).\n   292→\n   293→    Returns:\n   294→        t_score (adarray or scaler): dimension same as the '...' of x1 or x2.\n   295→        pval (adarray or scaler): dimension same as t_score\n   296→    \"\"\"\n   297→    n1 = x1.shape[-1]\n   298→    n2 = x2.shape[-1]\n   299→    df = n1 +n2 - 2 # degree of freedom\n   300→\n   301→    Sp = ( (n1-1)*np.var(x1,axis=-1) + (n2-1)*np.var(x2,axis=-1) ) / df\n   302→    s = np.sqrt(Sp*(1/n1 + 1/n2))\n   303→\n   304→    t_score = (np.mean(x2, axis=-1) - np.mean(x1, axis=-1)) / s # （n_bins,n_neurons）\n   305→    # if isinstance(a, np.array): # not scalar\n   306→    #     t_score[np.isnan(t_score)] = 0 # sometimes Sp=0 (since var=0). This is to deal with cases where for e.g. (bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride is actually 43 but evaluates to 42.999999... due to numerical errors in floating-point arithmetic.\n    45→    if np.allclose((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride, math.floor((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride)+1):\n    46→        n_bins =  math.floor((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride)+2\n    47→    else:\n    48→        n_bins = math.floor((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride)+1\n    49→    binCenters = bin_begin_time + np.arange(n_bins)*stride\n    50→\n    51→    binIntervals = np.vstack((binCenters-bin_width/2, binCenters+bin_width/2)).T\n    52→\n    53→    # Count number of spikes for each sliding bin\n    54→    binSpikes = np.asarray([[[np.sum(np.all([trial>=binInt[0], trial<binInt[1]], axis=0)) for binInt in binIntervals] for trial in unit]\n    55→        for unit in spikeTimes]).swapaxes(0,-1)\n    56→\n    57→    if rate:\n    58→        return binCenters, binSpikes/float(bin_width) # get firing rates\n    59→    return binCenters, binSpikes\n    60→\n    61→\n    62→def process_one_sess(data_folder, file_path_list, bw, stride, begin_time=-3.0, end_time=3.5, save_folder=os.path.join(os.pardir, 'data_processed'), qc_mode='classifier'):\n    63→    \"\"\"\n    64→    Process all probe files in one session.\n    65→\n    66→    Args:\n    67→        data_folder (str): the folder containing all data including QC neuron ID files.\n    68→        file_path_list: list of full file paths of all probes in this session.\n    69→        bw (float): in sec.\n    70→        stride (float): in sec.\n    71→        qc_mode (str): 'classifier' or 'old'.\n    72→    \"\"\"\n    73→    print(qc_mode)\n    74→    if qc_mode == 'classifier':\n    75→        qc_folder = os.path.join(data_folder, 'goodunits')\n    76→        print(qc_folder)\n    77→    elif qc_mode == 'old':\n    78→        qc_folder = os.path.join(data_folder, 'DJ_GoodUnitsIdx_14regions_old_qc')\n    79→    else:\n    80→        raise ValueError('Wrong qc_mode! For putative type, we have: 'Pyr' - pyramidal neurons, 'FS' - fast spiking, 'not classified', 'all' - all types\n   192→        probe_no_col = probe * np.ones((unit_info.shape[0], 1)) # (n_units,1) original probe #\n   193→        unit_info = np.concatenate([unit_info, probe_no_col], axis=1) # (n_units, 9)\n   194→        unit_qc = sess_file['neuron_unit_quality_control']\n   195→        # dictionary. Needed keys: 'unit_amp', 'presence_ratio', 'amplitude_cutoff', 'isi_violation', 'unit_snr', 'avg_firing_rate', 'drift_metric'. Values are all list with length n_neurons.\n   196→\n   197→        # -- 1.2.3 histology\n   198→        histology = sess_file['histology'] # dictionary, keys: 'unit', 'ccf_x', 'ccf_y', 'ccf_z', 'annotation'\n   199→        ccf_unit_id = np.array(histology['unit']) # ndarray, (n_neurons,)\n   200→        ccf_coordinate = np.stack([histology['ccf_x'], histology['ccf_y'], histology['ccf_z']]).swapaxes(0,1) # ndarray (n_neurons,3), xyz coordinates\n   201→        ccf_label = histology['annotation'] # list of np.str_\n   202→        ccf_label = [str(x).strip() for x in ccf_label] # list of string, with both whitespace at the beginning and end of each string removed\n   203→\n   204→        # -- 1.2.4 get units that have both ephys and histology\n   205→        unit_comb = set(unit_info[:,0]) & set(ccf_unit_id) # get the id of units that have both ephys and histology\n   206→        mask_ephys = [True if x in unit_comb else False for x in unit_info[:,0]] # mask for all other data (ephys) except histology\n   207→        mask_histology =[True if x in unit_comb else False for x in ccf_unit_id] # mask for histology only\n   208→\n   209→        # mask CCF info\n   210→        ccf_coordinate = ccf_coordinate[mask_histology,:] # ndarray (n_neurons,3), xyz coordinates\n   211→        ccf_label = list(compress(ccf_label, mask_histology)) # list of string, with both whitespace at the beginning and end of each string removed\n   212→        ccf_unit_id = ccf_unit_id[mask_histology]\n   213→\n   214→        # mask ephys info\n   215→        unit_info = unit_info[mask_ephys,:] # (n_neurons, 9)\n   216→        spike_times = spike_times[mask_ephys]\n   217→        for name in unit_qc.keys():\n   218→            unit_qc[name] = list(np.array(unit_qc[name])[mask_ephys])\n   219→\n   220→        # --- 1.3 add probe data to sess data ---\n   221→        spike_times_sess.append(spike_times) # ndarray. spike_times[neuron_id][trial_id] is ndarray\n   222→        unit_info_sess.append(unit_info) # (n_units, 9)\n   223→        unit_qc_sess_list.append(unit_qc) # dictionary. Needed keys: 'unit_amp', 'presence_ratio', 'amplitude_cutoff', 'isi_violation', 'unit_snr', 'avg_firing_rate', 'drift_metric'. Values are all list with length n_neurons.\n   224→        ccf_coordinate_sess.append(ccf_coordinate) # ndarray (n_neurons,3), xyz coordinates\n   225→        ccf_label_sess = ccf_label_sess + ccf_label # list\n   226→        ccf_unit_id_sess.append(ccf_unit_id) # ndarray, (n_neurons,)\n   227→\n   228→    # ----- 2. combine data from all probes ---\n   229→    spike_times_sess = np.concatenate(spike_times_sess) # ndarray. spike_times[neuron_id][trial_id] is ndarray\n   230→    unit_info_sess = np.concatenate(unit_info_sess, axis=0) # (n_units, 9)\n   231→\n   232→    # uncommented\n   233→    unit_qc_sess = {'unit_amp': [], 'presence_ratio': [], 'amplitude_cutoff': [], 'isi_violation': [], 'avg_firing_rate':[], 'drift_metric': []}\n   234→    for probe_qc_dict in unit_qc_sess_list:\n   235→        for qc_name in unit_qc_sess.keys():\n   236→            unit_qc_sess[qc_name] = unit_qc_sess[qc_name] + probe_qc_dict[qc_name]\n   237→\n   238→    ccf_coordinate_sess = np.concatenate(ccf_coordinate_sess, axis=0) # ndarray (n_neurons,3), xyz coordinates\n   239→    ccf_unit_id_sess = np.concatenate(ccf_unit_id_sess) # ndarray, (n_neurons,)\n   240→\n   241→    n_neurons = len(spike_times_sess)\n   242→    assert unit_info_sess.shape[0] == n_neurons\n   243→    assert len(unit_qc_sess['drift_metric']) == n_neurons\n   244→    assert ccf_coordinate_sess.shape[0] == n_neurons\n   245→    assert len(ccf_label_sess) == n_neurons\n   246→    assert len(ccf_unit_id_sess) == n_neurons\n   247→    # print('total neurons:', n_neurons)\n   248→\n   249→    # ----- 3. Process neurons from areas of interest and perform QC filtering -----\n   250→    i_file = 1\n   251→    neuron_id_used = []\n   252→    neuron_id_all = np.arange(n_neurons) # neuron_id is continuous from 0. unit_id is the original id from DJ.\n   253→\n   254→    for region in ['ALM', 'Medulla', 'Midbrain', 'Striatum', 'Thalamus', 'Pons', 'Cerebellum', 'Hypothalamus', 'Hippocampus', 'Orbital', 'OtherCortex', 'Olfactory', 'CorticalSubplate', 'Pallidum']:\n   255→        for side in ['left', 'right']:\n   256→            area = '_'.join([side, region])\n   257→            area_key = '{}_qc'.format(region)\n   258→            neuron_id_area = helper_get_neuron_id_area(idx_qc_dict[area_key], anno_qc_dict[area_key], ccf_coordinate_sess, ccf_label_sess, side, region)\n   259→\n   260→            if len(neuron_id_area) == 0:\n   261→                continue\n   262→\n   263→            print('Filtered neurons:', area, len(neuron_id_area))\n   264→\n   265→            # record used neurons\n   266→            neuron_id_used = neuron_id_used + neuron_id_area\n   267→\n   268→            save_flag = process_one_area(sess_name, area, neuron_id_area, i_file, bw, stride, begin_time, end_time, save_folder_sess, spike_times_sess, unit_info_sess, unit_qc_sess, ccf_coordinate_sess, ccf_label_sess, ccf_unit_id_sess, sess_dict)\n   269→\n   270→            if save_flag:\n   271→                i_file += 1\n   272→\n   273→    # ----- 4. Don't process the rest of the neurons -----\n   274→    with open(os.path.join(save_folder_sess, 'others_finished.txt'), 'w') as f:\n   275→        f.write('{}: finished.'.format(sess_name))\n   276→\n   277→    '''\n   278→    neuron_id_others = set(neuron_id_all) - set(neuron_id_used)\n   279→    neuron_id_others = sorted(list(neuron_id_others))\n   280→\n   281→    if len(neuron_id_others)==0:\n   282→        return\n   283→\n   284→    print('Filtered neurons:', 'others', len(neuron_id_others))\n   285→    process_one_area(sess_name, 'others', neuron_id_others, i_file, bw, stride, begin_time, end_time, save_folder_sess, spike_times_sess, unit_info_sess, ccf_coordinate_sess, ccf_label_sess, ccf_unit_id_sess, sess_dict)\n   286→    '''\n   287→\n   288→\n   289→def process_one_area(sess_name, area, neuron_id_area, i_file, bw, stride, begin_time, end_time, save_folder_sess, spike_times_sess, unit_info_sess, unit_qc_sess, ccf_coordinate_sess, ccf_label_sess, ccf_unit_id_sess, sess_dict):\n   290→    \"\"\"\n   291→    Get all neurons in an area, extract firing rates, and then save data.\n   292→    \"\"\"\n   293→    if os.path.exists(os.path.join(save_folder_sess, '%d_%s.pickle' % (i_file, area))):\n   294→        return True\n   295→\n   296→    # filter using the neuron mask\n   297→    n_units = unit_info_sess.shape[0]\n   298→    spike_times_area = spike_times_sess[neuron_id_area]\n   299→    unit_info_area = unit_info_sess[neuron_id_area,:] # (n_units, 9)\n   300→\n   301→    # uncommented\n   302→    unit_qc_area = {}\n   303→    for qc_name in unit_qc_sess.keys():\n   304→        unit_qc_area[qc_name] = np.array(unit_qc_sess[qc_name])[neuron_id_area]\n   305→        assert len(unit_qc_sess[qc_name]) == n_units\n   306→\n   307→    ccf_coordinate_area = ccf_coordinate_sess[neuron_id_area,:] # ndarray (n_neurons,3)\n   308→    ccf_label_area = list(np.array(ccf_label_sess)[neuron_id_area])\n   309→    ccf_unit_id_area = list(ccf_unit_id_sess[neuron_id_area])\n   310→\n   311→    # truncate spike times into desired time window (already aligned to go cue in raw data)\n   312→    for i_cluster, st_cluster in enumerate(spike_times_area):\n   313→        for i_trial, st_trial in enumerate(st_cluster):\n   314→            # if spike_times[i_cluster][i_trial] != []:\n   315→            #     amin_all.append(np.amin(spike_times[i_cluster][i_trial]))\n   316→            #     amax_all.append(np.amax(spike_times[i_cluster][i_trial]))\n   317→            if isinstance(st_trial, float) or isinstance(st_trial, int):\n   318→                spike_times_area[i_cluster][i_trial] = np.array([st_trial])\n   319→            # temp = spike_times[i_cluster][i_trial].copy()\n   320→            temp = spike_times_area[i_cluster][i_trial] # in Susu's data, spike_times are relative to go cue time\n   321→            spike_times_area[i_cluster][i_trial] = spike_times_area[i_cluster][i_trial][(temp>=begin_time)*(temp<=end_time)]\n   322→\n   323→    # bin spike times into firing rates\n   324→    bin_centers, fr = sliding_histogram(spike_times_area, begin_time, end_time, bin_width=bw, stride=stride, rate=True) # fr: (n_bins, n_trials, n_neurons)\n   325→\n   326→    if os.path.exists(os.path.join(save_folder_sess, '%d_%s.pickle' % (i_file, area))):\n   327→        with open(os.path.join(save_folder_sess, '%d_%s.pickle' % (i_file, area)), 'rb') as f: # HERE\n   328→            area_dict_exist = pickle.load(f)\n   329→            fr = area_dict_exist['fr']\n   330→            spike_times_area = area_dict_exist['spike_times']\n   331→            bin_centers = area_dict_exist['bin_centers']\n   332→\n   333→    # organize data into dict\n   334→    area_dict = {}\n   335→    area_dict['sess_name'] = sess_name # str\n   336→    area_dict['area'] = area # str\n   337→\n   338→    area_dict['bin_centers'] = bin_centers\n   339→    area_dict['fr'] = fr # (n_bins, n_trials, n_neurons)\n   340→    area_dict['spike_times'] = spike_times_area # ndarray. spike_times[neuron_id][trial_id] is ndarray with all spike times of this neuron in this trial. (go cue time is 0.)\n   341→    area_dict['neuron_info'] = unit_info_area # ndarray (n_units, 9)\n   342→    #  columns: unit_id, unit_quality, unit_x_in_um, depth_in_um, associated_electrode, shank, cell_type, recording_location, probe #. For putative type, we have: 'Pyr' - pyramidal neurons, 'FS' - fast spiking, 'not classified', 'all' - all types\n   343→    area_dict['unit_qc'] = unit_qc_area # dict. Keys are 'unit_amp', 'presence_ratio', 'amplitude_cutoff', 'isi_violation',, 'avg_firing_rate', 'drift_metric'. Values are all list with length n_neurons.\n   344→    area_dict['ccf_coordinate'] = ccf_coordinate_area # ndarray (n_neurons,3), xyz coordinates\n   345→    area_dict['ccf_label'] = ccf_label_area # list of string, with whitespace at the beginning and end of each string removed\n   346→    area_dict['ccf_unit_id'] = ccf_unit_id_area # ndarray (n_neurons,), id of units with histology\n   347→\n   348→    for name, info in sess_dict.items():\n   349→        area_dict[name] = info\n   350→\n   351→    # save data\n   352→    with open(os.path.join(save_folder_sess, '%d_%s.pickle' % (i_file, area)), 'wb') as f:\n   353→        pickle.dump(area_dict, f)\n   354→\n   355→    return True\n   356→\n   357→\n   358→\n   359→def helper_get_neuron_id_area(neuron_idx_list, anno_qc_list, ccf_coor, ccf_label, side, region):\n   360→    \"\"\"\n   361→    Filter the neurons in data_sess so that only neurons in side_region is left.\n   362→\n   363→    Args:\n   364→        neuron_idx_list (list): list of good neuron IDs.\n   365→        anno_qc_list (list): list of CCF annotations of good neurons.\n   366→        ccf_coor (ndarray): ndarray (n_neurons,3), xyz coordinates.\n   367→        ccf_label (list): list of string (CCF annotations), with length n_neurons.\n   368→        side (str): 'left' or 'right'.\n   369→        region (str): 'ALM', 'Medulla', 'Midbrain', 'Striatum', 'Thalamus', 'BLA', or 'ECT'.\n   370→\n   371→    Returns:\n   372→        neuron_id_area (list): id of neurons within side_region.\n   373→    \"\"\"\n   374→    if len(neuron_idx_list) == 0:\n   375→        return []\n   376→\n   377→    n_neurons = ccf_coor.shape[0]\n   378→    assert len(ccf_label) == n_neurons\n   379→    neurons_all = np.arange(n_neurons)\n   380→\n   381→    # filter neurons using side\n   382→    ML_mid_coor = 5700\n   383→    if side == 'left':\n   384→        mask_side = ccf_coor[:,0] >= ML_mid_coor\n   385→    elif side == 'right':\n   386→        mask_side = ccf_coor[:,0] < ML_mid_coor\n   387→    neuron_id_side = neurons_all[mask_side]\n   388→\n   389→    if len(neuron_id_side) == 0:\n   390→        return []\n   391→\n   392→    # combine side filter with qc filter\n   393→    neuron_id_area = sorted(list(set(neuron_id_side) & set(neuron_idx_list)))\n   394→    neuron_id_area = [idx for idx in neuron_id_area if ccf_label[idx] != []]\n   395→    if len(neuron_id_area) == 0:\n   396→        return []\n   397→    ccf_label_list = list(np.array(ccf_label)[neuron_id_area])\n   398→    ccf_label_joined = ''.join(ccf_label_list)\n   399→\n   400→    idx_side = [i for i, idx in enumerate(neuron_idx_list) if idx in neuron_id_area]\n   401→    anno_qc_list = list(np.array(anno_qc_list)[idx_side])\n   402→    anno_qc_joined = ''.join(anno_qc_list)\n   403→\n   404→    # temp = list(np.array(ccf_label)[neuron_id_area])\n   405→    # print(len(temp), len(anno_qc_list))\n   406→    # num = np.amin([len(temp), len(anno_qc_list)])\n   407→    # for ii in range(num):\n   408→        # if temp[ii] != anno_qc_list[ii]:\n   409→        # print(ii, temp[ii], anno_qc_list[ii])\n   410→    assert ccf_label_joined == anno_qc_joined\n   411→\n   412→    '''\n   413→    # filter neurons using region\n   414→    if region == 'ALM':\n   415→        res = 100 # resolution: 100 um\n   416→        # ALM_voxels = np.load(open('ALM_voxels_raw.npy', 'rb')) # (n_voxels,3), xyz of ALM\n   417→        ALM_voxels = np.load(open('ALM_voxels_symmetric.npy', 'rb')) # (n_voxels,3), xyz of ALM regions\n   418→\n   419→        diff = ccf_coor[:,None,:] - ALM_voxels[None,:,:] # (n_neurons, n_voxels, 3)\n   420→        mask_region = (np.abs(diff[:,:,0]) <= res/2) * (diff[:,:,1] <= res/2) * (np.abs(diff[:,:,2]) <= res/2) # (n_neurons, n_voxels)\n   421→        mask_region = np.any(mask_region, axis=1) # (n_neurons,)\n   422→\n   423→        neuron_id_region = neurons_all[mask_region]\n   424→    else:\n   425→        mask_region, neuron_id_region = get_id_subarea(region, ccf_label)\n   426→    '''\n   427→\n   428→    # neuron_id_area = sorted(list( set(neuron_id_side) & set(neuron_id_region)))\n   429→    return neuron_id_area\n   430→\n   431→\n   432→def helper_filter_by_neuron_id(data_sess, neuron_id_area, probe_no, area_name):\n   433→    \"\"\"\n   434→    Using the given neuron_id_area, filter the neuron sin data_sess.\n   435→\n   436→    Arg:\n   437→        data_sess (dict): same format as sess_dict.\n   438→        neuron_id_area (list): list of neuron if to use.\n   439→        probe_no (int): probe number.\n   440→        area_name (str):  name of the area. e.g. 'left_ALM'.\n   441→\n   442→    Returns:\n   443→        data_area (dict): same format as sess_dict.\n   444→    \"\"\"\n   445→    data_area = {}\n   446→\n   447→    data_area['mouse'] = data_sess['mouse']\n   448→    data_area['date'] = data_sess['date']\n   449→    data_area['probe'] = probe_no\n   450→    data_area['area'] = area_name\n   451→\n   452→    data_area['bin_centers'] = data_sess['bin_centers']\n   453→    data_area['fr'] = data_sess['fr'][:,:,neuron_id_area] # (n_bins, n_trials, n_neurons)\n   454→    data_area['spike_times'] = data_sess['spike_times'][neuron_id_area] # ndarray. spike_times[neuron_id][trial_id] is ndarray with all spike times of this neuron in this trial. (go cue time is 0.)\n   455→    data_area['auto_learn_trials'] = data_sess['auto_learn_trials'] # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n   456→    data_area['early_lick_trials'] = data_sess['early_lick_trials'] # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n   457→    data_area['auto_water_trials'] = data_sess['auto_water_trials'] # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n   458→    data_area['free_water_trials'] = data_sess['free_water_trials'] # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n   459→    data_area['lick_directions'] = data_sess['lick_directions'] # ndarray, (n_trials). Each element is a list of 0 (lick right) and 1 (lick left)\n   460→    data_area['lick_times'] = data_sess['lick_times'] # list with length n_trials, each element is 1D ndarray with all lick times in that trial\n   461→    data_area['gocue_time'] = data_sess['gocue_time']\n   462→    data_area['correctness'] = data_sess['correctness'] # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64. 1: correct or free water, 0: error, -1: no response\n   463→    data_area['neuron_info'] = data_sess['neuron_info'][neuron_id_area,:] # ndarray (n_units, 8)\n   464→\n   465→    data_area['ccf_coordinate'] = data_sess['ccf_coordinate'][neuron_id_area,:]  # ndarray (n_neurons,3), xyz coordinates\n   466→    data_area['ccf_label'] = list(np.array(data_sess['ccf_label'])[neuron_id_area]) # list of string, with whitespace at the beginning and end of each string removed\n   467→    data_area['ccf_unit_id'] = data_sess['ccf_unit_id'][neuron_id_area] # ndarray (n_neurons,), id of units with histology\n   468→\n   469→    data_area['probe_info'] = 'reorganized'\n   470→    data_area['delay_period'] = data_sess['delay_period'] # (n_trials,) duration of delay period\n   471→    data_area['sample_period'] = data_sess['sample_period'] # (n_trials,) duration of sample period\n   472→    data_area['stimulation'] = data_sess['stimulation'] # ndarray, (n_trials,4). The 4 columns are [laser_power, stim_type('1', '2', or '6', with 1,2,6 being left/right/both ALM perturbation), laser_on_time, laser_off_time]. For no stim trials, we have [0, NaN, NaN, NaN]\n   473→    data_area['trial_type'] = data_sess['trial_type'] # ndarray, (n_trials,). 1 is left, 0 is right 'r'\n   474→\n   475→    return data_area\n   476→\n   477→\n   478→def process_all_sess(bw, stride, begin_time, end_time, data_folder, save_folder, qc_mode):\n   479→    # scan through all sessions, and find files within the same session\n   480→    # all_path_list = sorted(list(glob.glob(os.path.join(data_folder, 'MAP_dandi', 'map*.mat')))) # SC065_20210507_172825_s8\n   481→    all_path_list = sorted(list(glob.glob(os.path.join(data_folder, 'MAP_dandi', 'map-export_SC050_20210302_140243_s20_p*.mat')))) # SC011_20190222_130111_s4\n   482→\n   483→    name2paths = OrderedDict() # keys are part of file names that are the same for all sessions. Values are list of full file paths in that sess.\n   484→    for filepath in all_path_list:\n   485→        filename = os.path.split(filepath)[-1]\n   486→        name = filename[:-7]\n   487→        if name not in list(name2paths.keys()):\n   488→            name2paths[name] = []\n   489→        name2paths[name].append(filepath)\n   490→\n   491→    for name, path_list in name2paths.items():\n   492→        print('Processing %s (%d files)' % (name, len(path_list)))\n   493→        process_one_sess(data_folder, path_list, bw, stride, begin_time, end_time, save_folder, qc_mode)\n   494→\n   495→\n   496→def process_all_sess_parallel(bw, stride, begin_time, end_time, data_folder, save_folder, qc_mode, n_cpu=multiprocessing.cpu_count()):\n   497→    # scan through all sessions, and find files within the same session\n   498→    all_path_list = sorted(list(glob.glob(os.path.join(data_folder, 'MAP_dandi', 'map*.mat')))) # SC065_20210507_172825_s8\n   499→\n   500→    name2paths = OrderedDict() # keys are part of file names that are the same for all sessions. Values are list of full file paths in that sess.\n   501→    for filepath in all_path_list:\n   502→        filename = os.path.split(filepath)[-1]\n   503→        name = filename[:-7]\n   504→        if name not in list(name2paths.keys()):\n   505→            name2paths[name] = []\n   506→        name2paths[name].append(filepath)\n   507→\n   508→    start_time = time.time()\n   509→    pool = multiprocessing.Pool(n_cpu)\n   510→    for name, path_list in name2paths.items():\n   511→        print('Processing %s (%d files)' % (name, len(path_list)))\n   512→        pool.apply_async(process_one_sess, args=(data_folder, path_list, bw, stride, begin_time, end_time, save_folder, qc_mode))\n   513→    pool.close()\n   514→    pool.join()\n   515→    print('The whole preprocessing took %.2f hours.' % ((time.time() - start_time)/3600))\n   516→\n   517→\n   518→\n   519→\n   520→if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n   521→    stride = 0.05\n   522→    bw = 0.1 # unit: sec\n   523→    begin_time = -3.0 # unit: sec\n   524→    end_time = 3.5\n   525→    qc_mode = 'classifier' # 'classifier' or 'old'\n   526→\n   527→    # data_folder = os.path.join(os.pardir, 'data','data_without_CCF') # Empty 0x1 cell. Need to manually define what it becomes in Python.\n2. 1x1 cell. Will be directly read as non-iterables such as str, int, float, etc. Need to manually convert it to an iterable with length 1.\n3. Nx1 cell (N >= 2). Usually it's fine to read and directly store it in Python.\n\nComment: in case 2, when using isinstance(data, data_type), if data could be a number, consider the possibility of data being either float or int, i.e. use\n    if isinstance(data, float) or isinstance(data, int):\n        do something\n\"\"\"\n\nplt.rcParams['pdf.fonttype'] = 42\nplt.rcParams['ps.fonttype'] = 42\n\n\ndef sliding_histogram(spikeTimes, begin_time, end_time, bin_width, stride, rate=True):\n    '''\n    Calculates the number of spikes for each unit in each sliding bin of width bin_width, strided by stride.\n    begin_time and end_time are treated as the lower- and upper- bounds for bin centers.\n    if rate=True, calculate firing rates instead of bin spikes.\n    '''\n    bin_begin_time = begin_time\n    bin_end_time = end_time\n    # This is to deal with cases where for e.g. (bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride is actually 43 but evaluates to 42.999999... due to numerical errors in floating-point arithmetic.\n    if np.allclose((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride, math.floor((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride)+1):\n        n_bins =  math.floor((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride)+2\n    else:\n        n_bins = math.floor((bin_end_time-bin_begin_time)/stride)+1\n    binCenters = bin_begin_time + np.arange(n_bins)*stride\n\n    binIntervals = np.vstack((binCenters-bin_width/2, binCenters+bin_width/2)).T\n\n    # Count number of spikes for each sliding bin\n    binSpikes = np.asarray([[[np.sum(np.all([trial>=binInt[0], trial<binInt[1]], axis=0)) for binInt in binIntervals] for trial in unit]\n        for unit in spikeTimes]).swapaxes(0,-1)\n\n    if rate:\n        return binCenters, binSpikes/float(bin_width) # get firing rates\n    return binCenters, binSpikes\n\n\ndef process_one_sess(data_folder, file_path_list, bw, stride, begin_time=-3.0, end_time=3.5, save_folder=os.path.join(os.pardir, 'data_processed'), qc_mode='classifier'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process all probe files in one session.\n\n    Args:\n        data_folder (str): the folder containing all data including QC neuron ID files.\n        file_path_list: list of full file paths of all probes in this session.\n        bw (float): in sec.\n        stride (float): in sec.\n        qc_mode (str): 'classifier' or 'old'.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(qc_mode)\n    if qc_mode == 'classifier':\n        qc_folder = os.path.join(data_folder, 'goodunits')\n        print(qc_folder)\n    elif qc_mode == 'old':\n        qc_folder = os.path.join(data_folder, 'DJ_GoodUnitsIdx_14regions_old_qc')\n    else:\n        raise ValueError('Wrong qc_mode! Each element is a list of 0 (lick left) and 1 (lick right)\n        lick_times = sess_file['behavior_lick_times'] # lick_times[trial_id] is ndarray, each element is a list of lick times (need to subtract go cue time from it)\n        correctness = sess_file['behavior_report'] # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64. 1: correct or free water, 0: error, -1: no response\n        gocue_time = sess_file['task_cue_time'] # ndarray, (2,n_trials). First row is go cue time, second row is duration of response period\n        delay_time = sess_file['task_delay_time'] # ndarray, (2,n_trials). First row is delay time, second row is duration of delay period\n        sample_time = sess_file['task_sample_time'] # ndarray, (2,n_trials). First row is sample time, second row is duration of sample period\n        delay_period = delay_time[1,:] # only need duration of delay period\n        sample_period = sample_time[1,:] # only need duration of sample period\n        stimulation = sess_file['task_stimulation'] # ndarray, (n_trials,4). The 4 columns are [laser_power, stim_type('1', '2', or '6', with 1,2,6 being left/right/both ALM perturbation), laser_on_time, laser_off_time] (with reference to trial start time - need to subtract go cue time from it).\n        trial_type = sess_file['task_trial_type'] # ndarray, (1,n_trials), 'l' or 'r'\n\n        # align stimulation time and lick times to gocue_time=0 #\n        lick_time_cal = []\n        for i_trial, lick_times_trial in enumerate(lick_times):\n            gocue_time_trial = gocue_time[0, i_trial]\n            lick_time_cal.append(lick_times_trial - gocue_time_trial)\n            stimulation[i_trial, 2:] -= gocue_time_trial\n        lick_times = lick_time_cal\n\n        if i_file == 0:\n            ### only save behavior and task info for the first probe file ###\n            sess_dict = {}\n            sess_dict['sess_name'] = sess_name\n            sess_dict['auto_learn_trials'] = auto_learn_trials\n            sess_dict['early_lick_trials'] = early_lick_trials # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n            sess_dict['auto_water_trials'] = auto_water_trials # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n            sess_dict['free_water_trials'] = free_water_trials # ndarray, (n_trials,), int64\n            sess_dict['lick_directions'] = lick_directions # ndarray, (n_trials). Keys are 'region_qc' (e.g. 'ALM_qc'). Values are list of good neuron IDs (in MATLAB it starts from 1 but here we change it to start from 0).\n            for area, id_list in qc_file['Idx'].items():\n                if id_list == []:\n                    idx_qc_dict[area] = []\n                elif isinstance(id_list, int) or isinstance(id_list, float): # only one neuron\n                    idx_qc_dict[area] = [id_list-1]\n                else:\n                    idx_qc_dict[area] = list(np.array(id_list) - 1)\n\n            anno_qc_dict = {}\n            for name, value in qc_file['AnnoName'].items():\n                if isinstance(value, str):\n                    anno_qc_dict[name] = [value]\n                else:\n                    anno_qc_dict[name] = value\n\n        # --- 1.2 neural data ---\n        # -- 1.2.1 spike times\n        spike_times = sess_file['neuron_single_units'] # ndarray. spike_times[neuron_id][trial_id] is ndarray\n\n        # -- 1.2.2 unit_info and QC stats\n        unit_info = sess_file['neuron_unit_info'] # ndarray (n_units, 8)\n        # neuron_unit_info: columns: unit_id, unit_quality, unit_x_in_um, depth_in_um, associated_electrode, shank, cell_type, recording_location. HERE\n            area_dict_exist = pickle.load(f)\n            fr = area_dict_exist['fr']\n            spike_times_area = area_dict_exist['spike_times']\n            bin_centers = area_dict_exist['bin_centers']\n\n    # (n_bins, n_trials, n_neurons)\n    area_dict['spike_times'] = spike_times_area # ndarray. spike_times[neuron_id][trial_id] is ndarray with all spike times of this neuron in this trial. (go cue time is 0.)\n    area_dict['neuron_info'] = unit_info_area # ndarray (n_units, 9)\n    #  columns: unit_id, unit_quality, unit_x_in_um, depth_in_um, associated_electrode, shank, cell_type, recording_location, probe #. For putative type, we have: 'Pyr' - pyramidal neurons, 'FS' - fast spiking, 'not classified', 'all' - all types\n    area_dict['unit_qc'] = unit_qc_area # dict. The default is 0.0034.\n   265→\n   266→    Returns\n   267→    -------\n   268→    joint_tt : np.ndarray\n   269→        The time stamps for the joint data.\n   270→    joint_embed_vecs : np.ndarray\n   271→        The embedding vectors aligned to the ephys data.\n   272→    joint_fr : np.ndarray\n   273→        The firing rates aligned to the embedding vectors.\n   274→    '''\n   275→    if ephys_tt[0] < embed_tt[0]:\n   276→        embed_start_ind = 0\n   277→        ephys_start_ind = find_closest(ephys_tt, embed_tt[0] + dt/2)\n   278→    else: \n   279→        embed_start_ind = find_closest(embed_tt, ephys_tt[0] - dt/2)\n   280→        ephys_start_ind = 0\n   281→\n   282→    if ephys_tt[-1] > embed_tt[-1] + dt/2:\n   283→        embed_end_ind = len(embed_tt)\n   284→        ephys_end_ind = ephys_start_ind + embed_end_ind - embed_start_ind\n   285→    else:\n   286→        ephys_end_ind = len(ephys_tt)\n   287→        embed_end_ind = embed_start_ind + ephys_end_ind - ephys_start_ind\n   288→\n   289→    joint_tt = ephys_tt[ephys_start_ind:ephys_end_ind]\n   290→    joint_embed_vecs = embed_vecs[embed_start_ind:embed_end_ind]\n   291→    joint_fr = fr[ephys_start_ind:ephys_end_ind]\n   292→\n   293→    return joint_tt, joint_embed_vecs, joint_fr\n   294→\n   295→def get_regular_trial_mask(ephys_data):\n   296→    '''\n   297→    Returns a mask for regular trials.\n   298→    \n   299→    No early lick, no auto water, no free water, no no response trials.\n   300→    '''\n   301→    return (ephys_data['early_lick_trials'] == 0) \\\n   302→         * (ephys_data['auto_water_trials'] == 0)  \\\n   303→         * (ephys_data['free_water_trials'] == 0)  \\\n   304→         * (ephys_data['correctness'] != -1) \\\n   305→         * (ephys_data['stimulation'][:,0] == 0)\n   306→\n   307→\n   308→def process_single_session_r2_dict(\n   309→        r2_dict, \n   310→        sample_period = [-1.85,-1.2], \n   311→        delay_period = [-1.2,0.], \n   312→        response_period = [0, 1.5],\n   313→        timeshift = 0):\n   314→    '''\n   315→    Get average R2 scores for sample, delay and response periods.\n   316→\n   317→    Calculates both the averages of the instantaneois R2 scores.\n   318→    And the R2 scores for all the trials and timepoints concatenated \n   319→    within an epoch. The default is [-1.2,0.].\n   329→    response_period : list, optional\n   330→        The response period. The default is [0, 1.5].\n   331→    timeshift : float, optional\n   332→        If there was timeshift between the video and firing rates.\n   333→        The default is 0.\n   334→\n   335→ for the sample period (old method).\n   345→    delay_r2_old : np.ndarray\n   346→        The average R2 scores for the delay period (old method).\n   347→    response_r2_old : np.ndarray\n   348→        The average R2 scores for the response period (old method).\n   349→\n   350→    '''\n   351→    dt = 0.0034\n   352→    tt = r2_dict['tt']\n   353→    r2_scores = r2_dict['r2_scores']\n   354→    y_test = r2_dict['y_test']\n   355→    y_pred = r2_dict['y_pred']\n   356→\n   357→    sample_mask = (tt >= sample_period[0]) * (tt < sample_period[1])\n   358→    sample_mask *= (tt >= (sample_period[0]-timeshift*dt)) * (tt < (sample_period[1]-timeshift*dt))\n   359→    delay_mask = (tt >= delay_period[0]) * (tt < delay_period[1])\n   360→    delay_mask *= (tt >= (delay_period[0]-timeshift*dt)) * (tt < (delay_period[1]-timeshift*dt))\n   361→    response_mask = (tt >= response_period[0]) * (tt < response_period[1])\n   362→    response_mask *= (tt >= (response_period[0]-timeshift*dt)) * (tt < (response_period[1]-timeshift*dt))\n   363→\n   364→    sample_r2 = np.mean(r2_scores[sample_mask,:], axis = (0,2))\n   365→    delay_r2 = Get the mean correlation and trial to trial variance for the firing rates.\n   470→\n   471→    Parameters\n   472→    ----------\n   473→    fr : np.ndarray\n   474→        The firing rates [time,trial,neuron].\n   475→\n   476→    Returns\n   477→    -------\n   478→    mean_corr : np.ndarray\n   479→        Mean correlation with the trial averaged firing rate. [neuron]\n   482→    '''\n   483→    trial_avg = fr.mean(axis = 1)\n   484→    mean_corr = np.zeros(fr.shape[-1])\n   485→    for i in range(fr.shape[-1]):\n   486→        mean_corr[i] = np.mean([np.corrcoef(trial_avg[:,i], fr[:,j,i])[0][1] for j in range(fr.shape[1])])\n   487→    \n   488→    trial_to_trial_var = np.mean(np.var(fr, axis = 1), axis = 0)\n   489→    return mean_corr, trial_to_trial_var\n   490→\n   491→\n   492→def calculate_selectivity(fr_R,fr_L, normalize = True):\n   493→    '''\n   494→    Returns the selectivity of each neuron.\n   495→\n   496→    Parameters\n   497→    ----------\n   498→    fr_R : np.ndarray\n   499→        The firing rates for the right trials [trials,neurons].\n   500→    fr_L : np.ndarray\n   501→        The firing rates for the left trials [trials,neurons].\n   502→    normalize : bool, optional\n   503→        If True, normalizes the selectivity by the standard deviations,\n   504→        resulting in d'. Otherwise, returns the difference in means. \n   505→        The default is True.\n   506→\n   507→    Returns\n   508→    -------\n   509→    selectivity : np.ndarray\n   510→        The selectivity for each neuron.\n   511→    '''\n   512→    mean_R = fr_R.mean(axis = 0)\n   513→    mean_L = fr_L.mean(axis = 0)\n   514→    std_R = fr_R.std(axis = 0)\n   515→    std_L = fr_L.std(axis = 0)\n   516→    if normalize:\n   517→        selectivity = (mean_R - mean_L) / np.sqrt((std_R**2 + std_L**2) / 2)\n   518→    else:\n   519→        selectivity = mean_R - mean_L\n   520→    return selectivity\n   521→\n   522→def calculate_auc(fr_R, fr_L, cv=5):\n   523→    '''\n   524→    Returns the AUC of each neuron.\n   525→\n   526→    Parameters\n   527→    ----------\n   528→    fr_R : np.ndarray\n   529→        The firing rates for the right trials [trials,neurons].\n   530→    fr_L : np.ndarray\n   531→        The firing rates for the left trials [trials,neurons].\n   532→    cv : int, optional\n   533→        The number of folds for cross-validation. Prepare data for the i-th neuron\n   553→        y = np.concatenate([np.ones(fr_R.shape[0]), np.zeros(fr_L.shape[0])])\n   554→        X = np.concatenate([fr_R[:, i], fr_L[:, i]]).reshape(-1, 1)\n   555→\n   556→        # The default is 5.\n\n    Returns\n    -------\n    auc : np.ndarray\n        The AUC for each neuron.\n    '''\n    from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\n    from sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold, GridSearchCV\n    from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\n    import numpy as np\n\n    # Initialize AUC array\n    auc = np.zeros(fr_R.shape[1])\n\n    # Define parameter grid for logistic regression\n    param_grid = {'C': [0.01, 0.1, 1, 10, 100]}\n\n    for i in range(fr_R.shape[1]):\n        # Prepare data for the i-th neuron\n        y = np.concatenate([np.ones(fr_R.shape[0]), np.zeros(fr_L.shape[0])])\n        X = np.concatenate([fr_R[:, i], fr_L[:, i]]).reshape(-1, 1)\n\n        # Outer CV loop\n        skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=cv)\n        fold_aucs = []\n\n        for train_index, test_index in skf.split(X, y):\n            X_train, X_test = X[train_index], X[test_index]\n            y_train, y_test = y[train_index], y[test_index]\n\n            # Inner CV for hyperparameter tuning\n            grid_search = GridSearchCV(\n                LogisticRegression(solver='liblinear'),\n                param_grid,\n                cv=cv,\n                scoring='roc_auc'\n            )\n            grid_search.fit(X_train, y_train)\n\n            # Best logistic regression model\n            best_model = grid_search.best_estimator_\n\n            # Evaluate on the test set\n            y_pred = best_model.predict_proba(X_test)[:, 1]\n            fold_aucs.append(roc_auc_score(y_test, y_pred))\n\n        # Average AUC across folds\n        auc[i] = np.mean(fold_aucs)\n\n    return auc\n","numLines":584,"startLine":1,"totalLines":584}}}

For each trial, tongue y-position samples are binned into the same 50ms bins as neural data. Within each bin, tongue y values are averaged. Bins with no tongue data are set to NaN.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef get_tongue_y_for_trial(tongue_ts, tongue_data, go_time, bin_centers=BIN_CENTERS, bin_width=BIN_WIDTH):\n    tongue_y = np.full(n_bins, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n    # ...\n    abs_bin_edges = go_time + bin_centers[0] - bin_width/2 + np.arange(n_bins + 1) * bin_width\n    bin_indices = np.digitize(local_ts, abs_bin_edges) - 1\n    for b in range(n_bins):\n        mask = bin_indices == b\n        if np.any(mask):\n            tongue_y[b] = np.mean(local_y[mask])\n    return tongue_y\n```\n\niii. Average within each time bin, using NaN for missing data.\n\n## 8-c. How is `output` *Tongue y-position* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. Per session, all valid (non-NaN) tongue y values across all trials are pooled. The 40th and 60th percentiles are computed. Values below 40th percentile become 0, between 40th-60th become 1, above 60th become 2. NaN time bins default to category 1 (middle).\n\nii.\n```python\ndef discretize_tongue_y(tongue_y_session, percentile_low=40, percentile_high=60):\n    all_values = []\n    for y in tongue_y_session:\n        valid = y[~np.isnan(y)]\n        if len(valid) > 0:\n            all_values.append(valid)\n    all_values = np.concatenate(all_values)\n    p_low = np.percentile(all_values, percentile_low)\n    p_high = np.percentile(all_values, percentile_high)\n\n    tongue_y_discrete = []\n    for y in tongue_y_session:\n        discrete = np.ones(len(y), dtype=np.int64)  # default middle\n        valid = ~np.isnan(y)\n        if np.any(valid):\n            discrete[valid & (y < p_low)] = 0\n            discrete[valid & (y >= p_low) & (y <= p_high)] = 1\n            discrete[valid & (y > p_high)] = 2\n        tongue_y_discrete.append(discrete)\n    return tongue_y_discrete\n```\n\niii. The instructions specify: 0 = <40th percentile, 1 = 40th-60th, 2 = >60th, with per-session discretization.\n\n## 8-d. How is `output` *Tongue y-position* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. The AI documented that 2,304 zero-data trials are not significantly correlated with specific trial characteristics and have minimal impact on training (2.8% of data).\n\n## 10-a. What are the most time-consuming steps of the code?\n\ni. According to timing information in the output, firing rate computation is the most time-consuming step, taking up to 7.1 seconds per session. Data loading takes 1.0-1.5 seconds per session. The full conversion takes approximately 19 minutes for 174 files.\n\nii.\n```python\nt_load = time.time()\nprint(f'  Data loaded in {t_load - t0:.1f}s')\nfr_all = compute_firing_rates_session(spike_times_good, valid_go_times)\nt_fr = time.time()\nprint(f'  Firing rates computed in {t_fr - t_load:.1f}s')\n```\n\niii. The AI documented timing estimates in CONVERSION_NOTES.md Step 7, showing firing rate computation as the dominant cost.\n\n## 10-b. What loops in the code could have been vectorized to improve efficiency?\n\ni. Several loops could be vectorized:\n- The inner loop over neurons in `compute_firing_rates_session` iterates per-neuron to compute histograms\n- The `get_tongue_y_for_trial` function loops over bins to compute mean tongue y\n- The `get_photostim_timeseries` function loops over start/stop pairs\n\nii.\n```python\n# Per-neuron loop in firing rate computation\nfor i, spikes in enumerate(spike_times_list):\n    if len(spikes) > 0:\n        counts, _ = np.histogram(spikes[left:right], bins=bin_edges)\n        fr[i, :] = counts / bin_width\n\n# Per-bin loop in tongue y\nfor b in range(n_bins):\n    mask = bin_indices == b\n    if np.any(mask):\n        tongue_y[b] = np.mean(local_y[mask])\n```\n\niii. The per-neuron histogram loop is the biggest efficiency concern, as `np.histogram` is called once per neuron per trial (n_neurons * n_trials times total). A vectorized bincount approach or bulk histogram could speed this up.\n\n## 10-c. What processing does the code repeat multiple times?\n\ni. The `BIN_CENTERS` and `time_from_tone` arrays are computed once globally and reused, which is efficient. However, `spike_times_all = nwb.units['spike_times'][:]` is read twice in `process_session` -- once for `get_recording_range` and once for extracting good unit spike times.\n\nii.\n```python\n# First read in get_recording_range:\ndef get_recording_range(nwb, good_indices):\n    spike_times_all = nwb.units['spike_times'][:]\n\n# Second read in process_session:\nspike_times_all = nwb.units['spike_times'][:]\n```\n\niii. Reading all spike times from the NWB file twice is redundant and wasteful, especially since this is a large data read from HDF5.\n\n## 10-d. What unnecessary processing does the code do that is discarded in downstream analyses?\n\ni. The `simplify_brain_region` function performs extensive string matching with a 60+ entry dictionary for every neuron, mapping detailed CCF annotations to abbreviations. While this is used in the output, the mapping is more elaborate than needed. The `subject_desc` field is extracted but only stored in metadata and not used for decoder training. Per session, all valid (non-NaN) tongue y values across all trials are pooled. The 40th and 60th percentiles are computed. Values below 40th percentile become 0, between 40th-60th become 1, above 60th become 2. NaN time bins default to category 1 (middle).\n\nii.\n```python\ndef discretize_tongue_y(tongue_y_session, percentile_low=40, percentile_high=60):\n    all_values = []\n    for y in tongue_y_session:\n        valid = y[~np.isnan(y)]\n        if len(valid) > 0:\n            all_values.append(valid)\n    all_values = np.concatenate(all_values)\n    p_low = np.percentile(all_values, percentile_low)\n    p_high = np.percentile(all_values, percentile_high)\n\n    tongue_y_discrete = []\n    for y in tongue_y_session:\n        discrete = np.ones(len(y), dtype=np.int64)  # default middle\n        valid = ~np.isnan(y)\n        if np.any(valid):\n            discrete[valid & (y < p_low)] = 0\n            discrete[valid & (y >= p_low) & (y <= p_high)] = 1\n            discrete[valid & (y > p_high)] = 2\n        tongue_y_discrete.append(discrete)\n    return tongue_y_discrete\n```\n\niii. What loops in the code could have been vectorized to improve efficiency?\n\ni. Several loops could be vectorized:\n- The inner loop over neurons in `compute_firing_rates_session` iterates per-neuron to compute histograms\n- The `get_tongue_y_for_trial` function loops over bins to compute mean tongue y\n- The `get_photostim_timeseries` function loops over start/stop pairs\n\nii.\n```python\n# Per-neuron loop in firing rate computation\nfor i, spikes in enumerate(spike_times_list):\n    if len(spikes) > 0:\n        counts, _ = np.histogram(spikes[left:right], bins=bin_edges)\n        fr[i, :] = counts / bin_width\n\n# Per-bin loop in tongue y\nfor b in range(n_bins):\n    mask = bin_indices == b\n    if np.any(mask):\n        tongue_y[b] = np.mean(local_y[mask])\n```\n\niii.
